# Project status

## Menu hubs and inner screens in one Style C look (2026-11-22)

All seven hub cards and the 33 inner screens now use the glossy Style C buttons in the wallet tones, with a pressed state. Hub cards carry a larger centred icon and vertically centred text. A menu panel fades in over 0.16 s by opacity only, so no geometry moves, and the fade is off with "Reduce effects". Energy, Profile and Settings no longer highlight an unrelated bottom tab.

Inner screens share one look. Building, skill, order and module cards and their actions use the same card style. Every screen has one heading in the same place, with the icon at the left of the title band; module details such as Bonus Reel and Salvage Module had sat 30 px to the left. Every back button reads "Back", where six different captions were used before.

A new `JtnFaceInset` keeps button labels on the flat face of the artwork, clear of its rim and bottom bevel, by changing only the text margins. Mini-game cards put their icon in the top-right corner and let the text use the full card height, so the four- and five-line cards still fit. Order cards centre their short text vertically. Settings opened from Profile now goes Back to Profile instead of closing the menu.

Checked on 1080x2340 and 750x1334 across 7 hubs and 33 inner screens: no text overflow, and no back caption other than "Back". Before the last mini-game card change, a glyph-by-glyph check found no label on the sprite rim at 1080x2340 and one at 750x1334 (Slot Level III); that check was not repeated afterwards. [Hubs](evidence/unity/menu-inner-style-20261122/hubs-before-after.png), [buildings and skills](evidence/unity/menu-inner-style-20261122/1-buildings-skills.png), [orders and modules](evidence/unity/menu-inner-style-20261122/2-orders-modules.png), [mini-games](evidence/unity/menu-inner-style-20261122/4-arcade-module.png), [750x1334](evidence/unity/menu-inner-style-20261122/8-750-memory-arcade.png).

The first full PlayMode run after these changes gave 176 passed, 3 failed and 2 skipped: three `ArcadeUiTests` cases found mini-game card text overflowing once the face insets applied. After the corner-icon change the final full two-pass run gave 179 passed, 0 failed, 0 Inconclusive and 2 skipped out of 181, the same as before this work. Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings and EditMode passed 16/16. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. Three PlayMode test files changed to follow the new captions and layout. [Verification](evidence/unity/menu-inner-style-20261122/verification.json).

Known leftovers: the order-card status stripe sits on the left rim of the artwork, the gloss shows faintly under the first letters of some cards, some labels are smaller because of the insets, and the mini-game cards lost their category caption. Not checked: a physical phone.

Next action is a check on a physical phone: text sizes, touch targets and vibration.

## Slot notes N4 to N6: wallet size, AUTO caption, SPIN label (2026-11-22)

The three wallets now share one text size: Coins, Diamonds and Energy all use 16, where they used 16, 15 and 13. Before House is repaired the AUTO button reads "after House" instead of "Repair House", so it no longer repeats the goal chip as if it were an action. The SPIN button reads "SPIN"; the duration appears only while the faster speed is active, as "SPIN · 1.5 s".

Checked on 1080x2340 and 750x1334 with no text overflow. [Before and after](evidence/unity/slot-n456-20261122/before-after-2340.png).

The full two-pass PlayMode run gave 177 passed, 1 failed, 1 Inconclusive and 2 skipped out of 181. The failure was a `FirstSessionPresentationUiTests` case that expected the old AUTO caption; its expectation now follows the new text and the class passes 23/25 on 1080x2340, the other two being the short-portrait cases that passed on 1080x2280 in the full run. The Inconclusive focus-dependent touch proof passed alone. The full run was not repeated after the expectation fix. Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings and EditMode passed 16/16. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. Not checked: a physical phone, and wallet text with very large balances. [Verification](evidence/unity/slot-n456-20261122/verification.json).

Next action is a check on a physical phone: text sizes, touch targets and vibration.

## Every enabled PlayMode case passes (2026-11-22)

The full PlayMode run now gives 179 passed, 0 failed, 0 Inconclusive and 2 skipped out of 181. On 461d479 it gave 165 passed, 12 failed, 2 Inconclusive and 2 skipped. Fourteen cases now pass and no case that passed now fails. The two skipped cases are the long end-to-end scenarios that need `JTN_RUN_FULL_E2E=1`.

One case exposed a game defect. Since the Style C pass on 2026-11-15 the ticket badge beside the reels ("TICKET IN N SPINS") drew through a clear surface that did not receive taps, so tapping it did nothing. It now opens Events again.

The other eleven repairs are test-only. Arcade information moved from the sixth hub card to the "Tickets & effects" secondary button, and a zero-reward finish reads "Finish · 0 Diamonds". The yard signature rounds to four decimals, because the selection ring sits on exact half-per-mille anchors that rounded either way after a resize. Two reflection calls pass the seventh `Funded` parameter, the repaired house shows the fourth of five stage sprites, and the inner-screen strip is the cream E6E1CF.

Four cases need no code change. They require the simulator to be preset to 1080x2280, and the longest-era case also needs `JTN_ERA_LONGEST_FAMILY`. The full run is therefore two passes: every case on 1080x2340, then these four by name on 1080x2280. The [run script](evidence/unity/playmode-repair-20261122/run-all-two-pass.sh) does both in the warm Editor in about eleven minutes and restores the simulator and environment.

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings and EditMode passed 16/16. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. Not checked: a physical phone. [Verification](evidence/unity/playmode-repair-20261122/verification-final.json).

Next action is a decision on the open slot design notes N4 to N6: the ENERGY wallet font size, the AUTO caption wording and "3.5 s" on SPIN.

## Full PlayMode baseline and repair of price, copy and legacy-button cases (2026-11-22)

One warm-Editor run of every PlayMode case now takes about eight to nine minutes: 181 cases under the `JunkToNeon.Tests.PlayMode` filter. On 4f5ba76 it gave 155 passed, 24 failed and 2 skipped. After this change it gives 165 passed, 12 failed, 2 Inconclusive and 2 skipped; twelve cases now pass and no case that passed now fails.

The repaired cases held three kinds of old expectation. Prices: V3 lowered House II from 540 to 480, Cart from 160 to 140 and Power Source from 200 to 190, so new selections read 480 and the completed-chapter wallet fixture is 10000-9310+372 = 1062. Copy: skill bodies read "Price: N Coins.", and the goal exchange opens the batch preview, which shows the wallet change instead of "Need N more coins". Routes: All Sections through Profile, Buildings through District, Skills through Upgrades, Expansion Home through Settings > Spin Speed, with Back paths adjusted for the nested hubs. `ExpansionUiTests` also follows the 2026-11-21 reel anchors.

One case exposed a game copy defect: the batch exchange preview said "need 1 coins." It now uses the singular for one Coin, like the single-trade preview.

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings and EditMode passed 16/16. The two Inconclusive cases are focus-dependent proofs in `CombinedLifecycleTests`: `FreshKeyboardSubmitUsesInstalledActionAndPaysWorkshopOnce` passed alone, and `RestoreConfirmationActualTouchRequiresNewConsentWhileStateIsNull` stayed Inconclusive alone after passing alone twice on 2026-11-21. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. Twelve cases still fail: four expect a 2280-pixel short portrait while the simulator stays at 2340, four are Arcade copy and one hit target, and four are single causes. [Verification](evidence/unity/playmode-repair-20261122/verification.json).

Next action is the short-portrait device switch shared by four cases, then the Arcade and single-cause cases.

## ReelUiLifecycle, ExpansionInput and DirectNavigation repaired (2026-11-21)

`ReelUiLifecycleTests` went from 9/18 to 18/18, `ExpansionInputTests` from 1/4 to 4/4 and `DirectNavigationUiTests` from 8/9 to 9/9. Hidden legacy root buttons were replaced by the product routes: House and Workshop through the yard buildings, Buildings through District > Construction, All Sections through Profile, Skills through Upgrades, and Expansion Home through Settings > Spin Speed. Behind those buttons the cases held older expectations, now aligned with current behaviour: yard and slot shares of .361 and .378 instead of .6 and .4, overlap checks only for legacy labels that are visible, the 480-Coin house improvement, the "old four-building version" notice, no extra card step after the goal plaque, Back from an order returning to the order list, and card captions compared as parsed text.

One case then found a game defect from the 2026-11-21 receipt change: with a Workshop bonus, three resources and a payment line, the text under the reels overflowed at the minimum font size. Resource amounts now join the receipt only when it is the single line.

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, EditMode passed 16/16, Premium 5/5 and `CombinedLifecycleTests` 43/43. Failing cases were run one by name and each suite once in full at the end. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. `FirstSessionPresentationUiTests` (6 of 25), `ArcadeUiTests` (4 of 14) and `InnerScreensUiTests` (1 of 1) were not rerun and stay non-passing; the full 189-case PlayMode run was not completed. [Verification](evidence/unity/playmode-repair-20261121/verification-reel-expansion-navigation.json).

Next action is one full PlayMode run kept as a baseline, then the FirstSession, Arcade and InnerScreens expectations.

## Recovery-screen exception fixed and CombinedLifecycle repaired (2026-11-21)

`StyleCQuickBoost` read `Session.Expansion` on every frame from `Update`. A blocked or recovering session throws there, so on the recovery screen the rest of `Update` did not run. The quick-boost icon now uses the last rendered bonus state. This predates the 2026-11-21 handoff.

`CombinedLifecycleTests` went from 25/43 to 42 passed with one focus-dependent Inconclusive, and 43 in one of three full runs. Twelve cases still pressed `projectButton`, `workshopButton` and the `moreProjectsButton` menu, hidden since the product navigation of 2026-11-01. House and Workshop now open through the yard buildings, which call the same `ShowChapterFamily`; the menu flows use the product tabs already used by newer cases, with a `Root()` helper for the deeper Back paths. Two stale strings were updated, and `ReopenedActionTouchProof` uses the yard buildings. The legacy "Chapter complete" guide from the old orders list has no product route, so three checks read the Progress panel and no longer assert the ending line, "Skills: n/6" or "no further era".

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, EditMode passed 16/16 and Premium 5/5. `RestoreConfirmationActualTouchRequiresNewConsentWhileStateIsNull` was Inconclusive in two full runs on Editor focus and passed alone twice. `PremiumReelsAndGoldShortPortrait` failed once inside `FirstSessionPresentationUiTests` and passed on rerun; that suite stays at 19/25. After the game fix `ReelUiLifecycleTests` is 9/18, `ExpansionInputTests` 1/4 and `ArcadeUiTests` 10/14. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. [Verification](evidence/unity/playmode-repair-20261121/verification.json).

Next action is to apply the same rerouting to ReelUiLifecycle, ExpansionInput and DirectNavigation, then review the FirstSession and Arcade expectations.

## Goal line and Guide stay put during a spin (2026-11-21)

During a spin the goal line keeps the last presented hint or stage summary instead of switching from a hint such as "Inspect the house. Viewing is free." to the summary and back. With the product chrome the Guide button stays visible with its normal look and is only non-interactable; the legacy layout still hides it. The busy status line shows "; paid N coins" only when N is above zero. A presented completion line keeps its separate retention, and rewards, saves and input rules are unchanged.

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, EditMode passed 16/16, and the five Premium and four ProductNavigation PlayMode cases passed. `FirstSessionPresentationUiTests` 19/25, `CombinedLifecycleTests` 25/43, `DirectNavigationUiTests` 8/9 and `ReelUiLifecycleTests` 8/18 match the previous run. A live spin on a private bonus-reel save in the 1080×2340 simulator read the same hint at rest, mid-spin and after, with Guide active and non-interactable mid-spin. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. The 17 non-passing CombinedLifecycle cases stop before their guide and heading steps, so that behaviour is only partly covered by tests. A phone was not checked. [Capture](evidence/unity/slot-spin-hint-20261121/midspin-before-rest-after.png) · [Verification](evidence/unity/slot-spin-hint-20261121/verification.json).

Next action is to repair or retire the stale PlayMode expectations, starting with CombinedLifecycle, so guide behaviour is covered again.

## Compact Slot header and larger reels (2026-11-21)

The Slot root uses a one-row header: "JUNK → NEON  Junkyard · 0/24" with the Parts/Potatoes strip inside it, so the strip no longer takes its own row. Everything below moved up and the slot block grew from 33% to 37.8% of the safe height; the yard keeps its size. On 1080×2340 a reel card grew from 302×400 to 321×462 px, the symbol box from 254 to 289 px and SPIN from 182 to 200 px. The symbol is limited by card width, so most of the gain comes from wider cards and smaller padding. `ApplyProductLayout` places the authored objects at runtime; the scene is unchanged. The quick-boost button is now placed on every refresh, because the authored one kept its old position and overlapped the goal plaque.

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, EditMode passed 16/16 and the five Premium PlayMode cases passed. `FirstSessionPresentationUiTests` 19/25, `CombinedLifecycleTests` 25/43, `ReelUiLifecycleTests` 8/18, `InnerScreensUiTests` 0/1 and `DirectNavigationUiTests` 8/9 have the same non-passing sets as before. `ExpansionInputTests` went from 0/4 to 1/4: its quick-boost case still expected "Bonus\nlocked", a text changed on 2026-11-21 without running that suite; the expectation now reads "Bonus" and the case passes with the button in its new place. Before and after were captured on 1080×2340, 1080×2280 and 750×1334 with no text overflow, including the longest header strings. On 750×1334 Profile and Settings are 98×85 px, about 42 pt. The normal save and backup kept their hashes. A phone and the four-card layout with the bonus reel unlocked were not checked. ## Dedicated large-win impact sound (2026-11-15)

A credited large win plays one 224 ms brass-edged thump when its expanded golden burst appears. The same threshold selects the wider burst and twelve sparks. Sound & Effects and the existing foreground guards own playback. The arrival sounds, wallet steps and committed rewards retain their prior timing.

The twelve-second Unity comparison shows consecutive +8 and +23 spins with both original Unity stereo mixes. The decoded MP4 contains zero large-win impacts and four Coin contacts on +8, and one large-win impact and six contacts on +23. Its large-win attack starts at 10.139 seconds, 11.3 ms before the encoded burst frame. The Unity audio callback and burst share the same capture frame. The full mix peaks below clipping. [Comparison MP4](evidence/unity/slot-large-win-audio-20261115/slot-win-audio-comparison.mp4) · [WAV](../Unity/Assets/Resources/Audio/Slot/large-win-impact.wav) · [Verification](evidence/unity/slot-large-win-audio-20261115/verification.json).

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, EditMode passed 16/16, and seven focused PlayMode cases passed, including silent +23 with effects disabled. The normal save and backup held exact bytes and timestamps during private runs. The ordinary save and portrait preview resumed with the Canvas Inspector locked. Next action is a listening review of the comparison.

## Coin counter steps and large-win burst (2026-11-15)

COINS displays the revealed balance as the coins reach the HUD icon. Each arrival adds its share and plays a quiet brass contact. The final value is the committed balance, including a simultaneous construction payment. AUTO can start the next spin while the previous coins finish arriving. The next outcome stays hidden until its own reveal, including on redraw. Modal, pause, reload and Sound & Effects cancellation settle the presentation without replaying it.

A credited +23 or greater uses twelve gold sparks and a broader, slower-fading burst. The ordinary +8 keeps four sparks. Two 1080×2340 Device Simulator spins recorded 37→39→41→43→45 and 37→41→45→49→53→57→60. All four ordinary and six large audio onsets matched the corresponding counter steps in the Unity mix. The four ordinary hits also matched after AAC encoding. [Six-second spin with sound](evidence/unity/coin-landing-20261115/slot-coin-landings.mp4) · [Side-by-side comparison](evidence/unity/coin-landing-20261115/slot-reward-comparison.mp4) · [Verification](evidence/unity/coin-landing-20261115/verification.json).

Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, Core passed 49 groups, EditMode passed 16/16, and eight focused PlayMode cases passed. The original save and backup held exact bytes and timestamps during private verification. The 239-Coin save resumed in the regular portrait preview. Next action is a visual and listening review of the two clips.

## Slot reel smoothing and Coin stream (2026-11-15)

The symbol strips use continuous acceleration through startup and braking, then compress and release at each stop. At positive Coin reveal, a brief burst stays at the reels while staggered Style C coins arc upward into the COINS icon. The icon pulses with a small glint on the last arrival and the amount lifts near the reels. The coin stream can finish during the next AUTO spin at the existing cadence. [Unity clip with sound](evidence/unity/coin-stream-20261115/slot-coin-stream.mp4) · [Verification](evidence/unity/coin-stream-20261115/verification.json).

Core passed 49 groups, Unity compiled with 0 errors and 0 warnings, EditMode passed 16/16 and eight focused PlayMode cases passed. The 60 fps capture records a live 37→45 spin and four distinct coins approaching the HUD. Regular and short Android simulator profiles confirm the landing pulse, inter-spin AUTO carry, reduced effects and lifecycle cancellation. The normal save and backup matched through private runs, and the main canvas and 209-Coin save resumed in the regular preview. Next action is to review the reward rhythm in the Unity preview.

## Android APK for the slot haptic check (2026-11-15)

Fresh Android ARM64 development APK `JunkToNeon-haptics-57811d1.apk` is 71,615,429 bytes. Unity compiled with no errors or warnings, all 49 Core groups passed and JunkToNeon EditMode passed 16/16. Gradle rejected a stale project-local transform on the first attempt. After quarantining that cache entry, the Unity-generated IL2CPP project packaged successfully through embedded Gradle. The new APK passed signature, alignment and ZIP integrity checks and contains the Android haptic bridge. Its certificate matches the previous debug APK. The normal save and backup matched their original bytes and timestamps, and the regular Editor preview was restored in Edit Mode. [Build verification](evidence/unity/slot-haptics-apk-20261115/verification.json).

ADB reported no connected devices. Next action is to install on a physical Android phone and check the three distinct light reel ticks and the stronger Coin confirmation with touch haptics enabled.

## Android haptic cues for the slot (2026-11-15)

Each reel stop now requests a light Android tick. Showing an accepted positive Coin receipt requests the stronger confirmation effect. Haptics share the **Sound & Effects** toggle with audio and visual feedback. The Android view uses the system touch preference, and queued cues are invalidated on pause, modal navigation, Reload and the next spin. [Implementation and phone check](evidence/unity/slot-haptics-20261115/README.md) · [Verification](evidence/unity/slot-haptics-20261115/verification.json).

Unity compiled the Android bridge with 0 errors and 0 warnings. Core 49 groups, EditMode 16/16 and six focused PlayMode cases passed. On both simulator portraits the observer saw three light requests and one confirmation for the seed-1 `+8` spin, with no replay after Reload. Effects-off and cancellation before reveal produced no request. The initial CLI HTTP 400 is retained with the successful result after readiness. Normal saves stayed byte and timestamp exact during private verification and the regular preview was restored. Next action is to check the distinct feel of both patterns on a physical Android phone.

## Slot roll, reel stops and Coin sound (2026-11-15)

The slot plays a light ratchet that slows into the final stop, one click at each of the three reel stops, and a brass chime at the visible reveal of a positive Coin receipt. The clicks use the reel stop thresholds and stereo positions. Settings now calls the existing session option **Sound & Effects**. The slot clears playback on pause, a modal, Reload or effects-off. [Live Unity MP4 with stereo sound](evidence/unity/slot-sound-20261115/slot-sound.mp4) · [Sound sources](../art_source/jtn/2026-11-15-slot-sound/README.md) · [Verification](evidence/unity/slot-sound-20261115/verification.json).

The six-second Device Simulator clip captures 180 rendered frames and Unity's own main audio mix. The seed-1 pointer spin reaches 45 Coins from 37 once. Three recorded clicks and the chime match their visual events within 20 ms in the decoded MP4. The first audio probe searched too narrow a window for the middle click and was corrected against the recorded PCM timing. Final compile 0 errors/0 warnings. Core 49 groups, EditMode 16/16 and six focused PlayMode cases passed. The normal saves stayed byte and timestamp exact during private verification. The ordinary preview resumed with the regular Simulator and locked Canvas Inspector.

## Generated Style C slot VFX and live clip (2026-11-15)

### Save-recovery backlog

Source inspection confirms the read-only finding: GameStore.Open validates an available backup but decodes the primary and blocks on corruption; JunkGame.Reload merely repeats OpenSession, and Error exposes only Retry. A valid backup therefore has no player-facing restore action. Keep this outside the presentation APK change. Small next fix: an explicit restore-from-validated-backup button with a rollback explanation, preserve corrupt primary and backup in bounded evidence slots, block if preservation slots conflict, then atomically promote the selected validated snapshot and use existing pending-operation recovery. Never silently reset or substitute a backup; rollback also rolls back sequence history, so exactly-once guarantees cannot span it. Test only private corrupted fixtures. No online backend is needed for this offline prototype.
